Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the complex structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 437. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 68 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_437(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 437. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^68(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
